Using a detailed case study with cross-encoder rerankers, we demonstrate that performance follows a predictable power law. This regularity allows us to accurately forecast the performance of larger models for some metrics more than others using smaller-scale experiments, offering a robust methodology for saving significant computational resources. For example, we accurately estimate the NDCG of a 1B-parameter model by training and evaluating only smaller models (up to 400M parameters), in both in-domain as well as out-of-domain settings. Our experiments encompass span several loss functions, models and metrics and demonstrate that downstream metrics like NDCG, MAP (Mean Avg Precision) show reliable scaling behavior and can be forecasted accurately at scale, while highlighting the limitations of metrics like Contrastive Entropy and MRR (Mean Reciprocal Rank) which do not follow predictable scaling behavior in all instances. Our results establish scaling principles for reranking and provide actionable insights for building industrial-grade retrieval systems.

</details>


### [191] [Detecting RAG Advertisements Across Advertising Styles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04925)
*Sebastian Heineking,Wilhelm Pertsch,Ines Zelch,Janek Bevendorff,Benno Stein,Matthias Hagen,Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM中生成的嵌入式广告检测，建立了广告风格分类体系，评估了不同检测方法在广告风格变化下的鲁棒性，发现基于实体识别的模型效果最好但轻量级模型易受风格变化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在检索增强生成(RAG)系统中应用，有机回答与上下文相关广告混合的"生成式原生广告"成为可能。现有数据集未能反映营销文献中讨论的广告风格多样性，需要研究自动检测这类广告的方法及其鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 为LLM开发广告风格分类体系，结合明确性和诉求类型两个维度；2) 模拟广告商可能通过改变广告风格来规避检测；3) 评估多种广告检测方法在这些风格变化下的鲁棒性，包括基于实体识别的模型和轻量级模型。

Result: 基于实体识别的模型在检测含广告的响应方面非常有效，且对广告风格变化具有较好的鲁棒性。然而，轻量级模型（如随机森林和SVM）在风格变化下表现脆弱，突显了实际应用中需要进一步研究效率导向的方法。

Conclusion: 虽然基于实体识别的模型能有效检测生成式广告且鲁棒性较好，但考虑到广告拦截将在资源受限的终端设备上进行，轻量级模型的脆弱性表明需要进一步研究兼顾效率和鲁棒性的实用广告拦截方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable a new form of advertising for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in which organic responses are blended with contextually relevant ads. The prospect of such "generated native ads" has sparked interest in whether they can be detected automatically. Existing datasets, however, do not reflect the diversity of advertising styles discussed in the marketing literature. In this paper, we (1) develop a taxonomy of advertising styles for LLMs, combining the style dimensions of explicitness and type of appeal, (2) simulate that advertisers may attempt to evade detection by changing their advertising style, and (3) evaluate a variety of ad-detection approaches with respect to their robustness under these changes. Expanding previous work on ad detection, we train models that use entity recognition to exactly locate an ad in an LLM response and find them to be both very effective at detecting responses with ads and largely robust to changes in the advertising style. Since ad blocking will be performed on low-resource end-user devices, we include lightweight models like random forests and SVMs in our evaluation. These models, however, are brittle under such changes, highlighting the need for further efficiency-oriented research for a practical approach to blocking of generated ads.

</details>


### [192] [Debiasing Sequential Recommendation with Time-aware Inverse Propensity Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04986)
*Sirui Huang,Jing Long,Qian Li,Guandong Xu,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TIPS方法，通过时间感知的逆倾向评分解决序列推荐中的曝光偏差问题，提升推荐性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐方法主要依赖显式交互（点击/购买），忽略了物品曝光信息，导致选择偏差和曝光偏差。需要区分"未曝光"和"不感兴趣"的物品，但无法从历史数据相关性中可靠推断。

Method: 提出时间感知逆倾向评分（TIPS）方法，与传统静态IPS不同，TIPS能有效考虑序列依赖性和时间动态性，更准确地捕捉用户偏好。

Result: 大量实验表明，TIPS作为各种序列推荐器的插件，能持续提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: TIPS通过解决序列推荐中的曝光偏差问题，提供了一种有效的反事实推理方法，显著改善了推荐系统的准确性。

Abstract: Sequential Recommendation (SR) predicts users next interactions by modeling the temporal order of their historical behaviors. Existing approaches, including traditional sequential models and generative recommenders, achieve strong performance but primarily rely on explicit interactions such as clicks or purchases while overlooking item exposures. This ignorance introduces selection bias, where exposed but unclicked items are misinterpreted as disinterest, and exposure bias, where unexposed items are treated as irrelevant. Effectively addressing these biases requires distinguishing between items that were "not exposed" and those that were "not of interest", which cannot be reliably inferred from correlations in historical data. Counterfactual reasoning provides a natural solution by estimating user preferences under hypothetical exposure, and Inverse Propensity Scoring (IPS) is a common tool for such estimation. However, conventional IPS methods are static and fail to capture the sequential dependencies and temporal dynamics of user behavior. To overcome these limitations, we propose Time aware Inverse Propensity Scoring (TIPS). Unlike traditional static IPS, TIPS effectively accounts for sequential dependencies and temporal dynamics, thereby capturing user preferences more accurately. Extensive experiments show that TIPS consistently enhances recommendation performance as a plug-in for various sequential recommenders. Our code will be publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [193] [Core-based Hierarchies for Efficient GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05207)
*Jakir Hossain,Ahmet Erdem Sarıyüce*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出用k-core分解替代Leiden聚类来改进GraphRAG，解决稀疏知识图中社区检测的非可重复性问题，通过确定性层次结构和轻量级启发式方法提升全局意义构建任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量的RAG方法在处理需要跨多个文档推理的全局意义构建任务时表现不佳。GraphRAG通过知识图组织文档，但当前基于Leiden聚类的方法在稀疏知识图上存在非可重复性问题，因为模块度优化存在指数级数量的近最优分区。

Method: 提出用k-core分解替代Leiden聚类，k-core在稀疏图上能产生确定性的密度感知层次结构。引入轻量级启发式方法，利用k-core层次构建大小受限、保持连通性的社区用于检索和摘要，以及基于token预算的采样策略降低LLM成本。

Result: 在真实世界数据集（财务收益电话会议记录、新闻文章、播客）上评估，使用三个LLM生成答案和五个独立LLM法官进行头对头评估。结果表明，该方法在所有数据集和模型上一致提高了答案的全面性和多样性，同时减少了token使用量。

Conclusion: 基于k-core的GraphRAG是一个有效且高效的全局意义构建框架，通过确定性社区检测和优化策略，在保持性能的同时降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge. However, existing vector-based methods often fail on global sensemaking tasks that require reasoning across many documents. GraphRAG addresses this by organizing documents into a knowledge graph with hierarchical communities that can be recursively summarized. Current GraphRAG approaches rely on Leiden clustering for community detection, but we prove that on sparse knowledge graphs, where average degree is constant and most nodes have low degree, modularity optimization admits exponentially many near-optimal partitions, making Leiden-based communities inherently non-reproducible. To address this, we propose replacing Leiden with k-core decomposition, which yields a deterministic, density-aware hierarchy in linear time. We introduce a set of lightweight heuristics that leverage the k-core hierarchy to construct size-bounded, connectivity-preserving communities for retrieval and summarization, along with a token-budget-aware sampling strategy that reduces LLM costs. We evaluate our methods on real-world datasets including financial earnings transcripts, news articles, and podcasts, using three LLMs for answer generation and five independent LLM judges for head-to-head evaluation. Across datasets and models, our approach consistently improves answer comprehensiveness and diversity while reducing token usage, demonstrating that k-core-based GraphRAG is an effective and efficient framework for global sensemaking.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [How Professional Visual Artists are Negotiating Generative AI in the Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04537)
*Harry H. Jiang,Jordan Taylor,William Agnew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对378名专业视觉艺术家的调查显示，大多数艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，通过多种拒绝策略抵制其在工作场所的应用，并报告了AI带来的负面职业影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在流行媒体和HCI研究中受到艺术家批评，但缺乏对其如何影响专业艺术家工作场所和职业的系统研究。本研究旨在填补这一空白，深入了解生成式AI对专业视觉艺术家职业生涯和工作环境的实际影响。

Method: 对378名经过验证的专业视觉艺术家进行问卷调查，研究生成式AI（文本或视觉）如何影响他们的职业生涯和工作场所。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 大多数视觉艺术家强烈反对使用生成式AI，并通过多种拒绝策略抵制其在工作场所的应用；(2) 艺术家使用生成式AI的决策受到客户、老板和同行压力等多种环境因素的影响；(3) 艺术家普遍报告生成式AI对他们的工作场所产生负面影响，导致压力增加和工作机会减少。

Conclusion: HCI研究者应更深入地考虑艺术家不希望在工作场所使用生成式AI的意愿，并关注AI技术对艺术职业生态的复杂影响。

Abstract: Generative AI has been heavily critiqued by artists in both popular media and HCI scholarship. However, more work is needed to understand the impacts of generative AI on professional artists' workplaces and careers. In this paper, we conduct a survey of \textit{378 verified professional visual artists} about how generative AI has impacted their careers and workplaces. We find (1) most visual artists are strongly opposed to using generative AI (text or visual) and negotiate their inclusion in the workplace through a variety of \textit{refusal} strategies (2) there exist a range of factors in artists environments shaping their use of generative AI, including pressure from clients, bosses, and peers and (3) visual artists report overwhelmingly negative impacts of generative AI on their workplaces, leading to added stress and reduced job opportunities. In light of these findings, we encourage HCI researchers to contend more deeply with artists' desires not to use generative AI in the workplace.

</details>


### [195] [Beyond the Interface: Redefining UX for Society-in-the-Loop AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04552)
*Nahal Mafi,Sahar Maleki,Babak Rahimi Ardabili,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个评估AI人机协同系统的社会技术框架，将用户体验从传统的前端可用性扩展到后端性能、组织工作流和决策结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用户体验框架是为确定性系统设计的，无法捕捉AI人机协同系统中概率性输出和人类参与带来的复杂社会技术动态。需要新的评估框架来理解AI系统在决策关键环境中的实际影响。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）对269个利益相关者见解进行归纳性社会建构分析；2）部署一个操作性的HITL视频异常检测系统进行实验研究。

Result: 研究发现利益相关者通过风险、治理和组织能力等多维度体验AI；实验结果显示检测行为和警报路由直接影响人类监督和工作量。基于此提出了四个社会技术指标：准确性（FPR/FNR）、操作延迟（响应时间）、适应时间（部署负担）和信任（验证的自动化量表）。

Conclusion: 该研究形式化了一个新的评估框架，将用户体验重新定义为跨越基础设施和治理的多层结构，为评估嵌入复杂现实世界生态系统的AI系统提供了严谨基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly operate in decision-critical environments where probabilistic outputs and Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) interactions reshape user engagement. Traditional user experience (UX) frameworks, designed for deterministic systems, fail to capture these evolving sociotechnical dynamics. This paper argues that in AI-enabled HITL systems, UX must transcend frontend usability to encompass backend performance, organizational workflows, and decision making structures.
  We employ a mixed-methods approach, combining an inductive social construction analysis of 269 stakeholder insights with the deployment of an operational HITL video anomaly detection system. Our findings reveal that stakeholders experience AI through multifaceted themes: risk, governance, and organizational capacity. Experimental results further demonstrate how detection behavior and alert routing directly calibrate human oversight and workload. Grounded in these results, we formalize a new evaluative framework centered on four sociotechnical metrics: Accuracy (FPR/FNR), Operational Latency (response time), Adaptation Time (deployment burden), and Trust (validated automation scales). This framework redefines UX as a multi-layered construct spanning infrastructure and governance, providing a rigorous foundation for evaluating AI systems embedded within complex real-world ecosystems.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond Anthropomorphism: a Spectrum of Interface Metaphors for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04613)
*Jianna So,Connie Cheng,Sonia Krishna Murthy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出将拟人化作为设计变量，开发从"反拟人化"到"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系，以揭示LLMs作为社会技术系统的本质，促进批判性参与而非单纯优化可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs界面过度强化拟人化隐喻，用户常将其视为真人，导致错觉和伤害。拟人化界面突出了LLMs与人类的相似性，却掩盖了关键差异，且缺乏保障措施。用户在使用伦理、技术普及与界面替代方案之间存在认知失调。

Method: 将拟人化重新定位为设计变量，构建从透明度驱动的"反拟人化"到怪异的"超拟人化"的隐喻谱系。这些隐喻为界面隐喻如何塑造和颠覆默认隐喻提供理论框架，通过引入物质性揭示LLMs作为由人类劳动、基础设施和数据塑造的社会技术系统。

Result: 提出的隐喻谱系将界面设计从优化可用性转向鼓励批判性参与。通过极端化拟人化设计，暴露LLMs的非人类本质和社会技术属性，帮助用户建立更准确的心理模型。

Conclusion: 拟人化应被视为可调节的设计变量而非默认设置。通过开发从反拟人化到超拟人化的隐喻谱系，可以打破默认拟人化隐喻，促进用户对LLMs社会技术本质的批判性理解，减少错觉和潜在伤害。

Abstract: Anthropomorphizing conversational technology is a natural human tendency. Today, the anthropomorphic metaphor is overly reinforced across intelligent tools. Large Language Models (LLMs) are particularly anthropomorphized through interface design. While metaphors are inherently partial, anthropomorphic interfaces highlight similarities between LLMs and humans, but mask crucial differences. As a result, the metaphor is often taken literally; users treat LLMs as if they are truly human. With few safeguards in place, this extreme anthropomorphism drives users to delusion and harm. Users also experience dissonance between the ethics of using LLMs, their growing ubiquity, and limited interface alternatives. We propose repositioning anthropomorphism as a design variable, developing opposing extremes as a theoretical framework for how interface metaphors shape and can disrupt the default metaphor. We introduce a spectrum of metaphors from transparency-driven ''anti-anthropomorphism'' to uncanny ''hyper-anthropomorphism''. These metaphors introduce materiality to interface metaphors, exposing LLMs as sociotechnical systems shaped by human labor, infrastructure, and data. This spectrum shifts interface design away from optimizing usability and toward encouraging critical engagement.

</details>


### [197] [Gamified Informed Decision-Making for Performance-Aware Design by Non-Experts: An Exoskeleton Design Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04643)
*Arman Khalilbeigi Khameneh,Armin Mostafavi,Alicia Nahmad Vazquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种游戏化的决策支持框架，将游戏引擎与实时性能反馈相结合，帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间，并在外骨骼立面改造项目中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策支持系统在帮助非专业设计师探索复杂的性能驱动设计空间方面具有重要作用。传统设计工具往往缺乏实时反馈和直观交互，限制了非专业人士对结构、环境、制造等多重性能标准的理解和权衡。需要开发一种更易用、直观的设计框架来促进知情决策。

Method: 开发了一个游戏化的决策支持框架，集成游戏引擎提供实时性能反馈。性能标准包括结构行为（挠度、质量、应力/强度比）、环境参数（太阳得热、供暖/制冷能耗）和制造考虑（制造成本、材料成本、机器人加工、材料设置）。该框架在建筑外立面改造项目中进行了测试，24名参与者在修改立面几何形状时能获得三个关键标准的实时反馈。

Result: 评估显示，游戏化反馈机制显著增强了用户对性能标准的理解和知情决策能力。参与者对结构、材料和制造性能的理解表明，经过策划的设计空间和结构化指导相比开放式生成工具提高了效率。参与者能够通过定制化谈判性能标准来参与设计过程。

Conclusion: 游戏化环境通过知情决策和性能标准的定制化谈判，使利益相关者能够参与设计过程。该研究为预占用评估做出了贡献，展示了游戏化框架在促进多标准设计决策方面的潜力，特别是在建筑改造等复杂设计任务中。

Abstract: Decision Support Systems (DSS) play a crucial role in enabling non-expert designers to explore complex, performance-driven design spaces. This paper presents a gamified decision-making framework that integrates game engines with real-time performance feedback. Performance criteria include structural behavior, environmental parameters, fabrication, material, and cost considerations. The developed design framework was tested with architecture students and non-expert designers on the design of an exoskeleton facade to retrofit an existing building. Participants (N=24) were able to iteratively modify façade geometries while receiving real-time feedback across the three key criteria: 1) structural behavior, including deflection, mass, and stress/strength ratio; 2) environmental parameters, such as solar gain and heating/cooling energy demands; and 3) fabrication considerations, including fabrication and material costs, robotic machining, and material setup. The evaluation of participant interactions reveals that gamified feedback mechanisms significantly enhance user comprehension and informed decision-making across the criteria. Further, participants' understanding of structural, material, and fabrication performance in relation to the iterative design task suggests that curated design spaces and structured guidance improve efficiency compared to open-ended generative tools. This research contributes to pre-occupancy evaluations, demonstrating how gamified environments enable stakeholder participation in the design process through informed decisionmaking and customized negotiation of performance criteria. .

</details>


### [198] [VizCrit: Exploring Strategies for Displaying Computational Feedback in a Visual Design Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04754)
*Mingyi Li,Mengyi Chen,Sarah Luo,Yining Cao,Haijun Xia,Maitraye Das,Steven P. Dow,Jane L. E*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizCrit系统通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成，为设计新手提供可操作性反馈，研究发现解决方案导向的反馈能减少设计问题并提升自我感知创造力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉设计教学中常提供多模态反馈（标注+文本），但反馈的"可操作性"存在差异（从概念提示到具体解决方案）。需要研究如何通过创意工具实现支持这种反馈的标注，以及反馈可操作性如何影响新手的设计过程、创造力感知、设计原则学习和最终成果

Method: 开发VizCrit系统，通过算法问题检测和视觉标注生成提供计算反馈，支持可操作性谱系。进行组间实验（N=36），新手在三种条件下修改设计：基于教材的反馈、意识导向反馈、解决方案导向反馈

Result: 解决方案导向反馈相比基于教材的反馈能减少更多设计问题，并带来更高的自我感知创造力，但专家对创造力的评分没有显著差异

Conclusion: AI创意支持工具应校准反馈可操作性，帮助新手在生产力与学习、成长、设计意识发展之间取得平衡。解决方案导向反馈对新手更有效，但需考虑其对创造力发展的长期影响

Abstract: Visual design instructors often provide multi-modal feedback, mixing annotations with text. Prior theory emphasizes the importance of actionable feedback, where "actionability" lies on a spectrum--from surfacing relevant design concepts to suggesting concrete fixes. How might creativity tools implement annotations that support such feedback, and how does the actionability of feedback impact novices' process-related behaviors, perceptions of creativity, learning of design principles, and overall outcomes? We introduce VizCrit, a system for providing computational feedback that supports the actionability spectrum, realized through algorithmic issue detection and visual annotation generation. In a between-subjects study (N=36), novices revised a design under one of three conditions: textbook-based, awareness-centered, or solution-centered feedback. We found that solution-centered feedback led to fewer design issues and higher self-perceived creativity compared with textbook-based feedback, although expert ratings on creativity showed no significant differences. We discuss the implications for AI in Creativity Support Tools, including the potential of calibrating feedback actionability to help novices balance productivity with learning, growth, and developing design awareness.

</details>


### [199] [Can LLMs Synthesize Court-Ready Statistical Evidence? Evaluating AI-Assisted Sentencing Bias Analysis for California Racial Justice Act Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04804)
*Aparna Komarla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发Redo.io开源平台，利用LLM分析加州监狱记录，自动生成法庭可用的种族偏见统计证据，以解决再判决中的"二次机会差距"问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管加州通过了《种族正义法案》(2020)，允许被告基于种族差异统计证据挑战定罪，但政策实施滞后于立法意图，导致数百个再判决机会未被识别，形成"二次机会差距"

Method: 开发Redo.io开源平台，处理通过《加州公共记录法》获取的95,000份监狱记录，使用LLM驱动的解释层将胜算比、相对风险、卡方检验等统计方法的结果合成为具有置信区间、样本量和数据限制上下文的连贯叙述

Result: 平台能够生成法庭就绪的种族偏见统计证据，用于初步证据和发现动议。通过LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估，LLM表现与统计学家相当，表明AI可以作为实时证据生成的有力描述性助手

Conclusion: 当在分析流程中合乎伦理地整合时，AI可以作为实时证据生成的有力描述性助手，帮助识别再判决机会，缩小"二次机会差距"，促进种族正义法案的有效实施

Abstract: Resentencing in California remains a complex legal challenge despite legislative reforms like the Racial Justice Act (2020), which allows defendants to challenge convictions based on statistical evidence of racial disparities in sentencing and charging. Policy implementation lags behind legislative intent, creating a 'second-chance gap' where hundreds of resentencing opportunities remain unidentified. We present Redo.io, an open-source platform that processes 95,000 prison records acquired under the California Public Records Act (CPRA) and generates court-ready statistical evidence of racial bias in sentencing for prima facie and discovery motions. We explore the design of an LLM-powered interpretive layer that synthesizes results from statistical methods like Odds Ratio, Relative Risk, and Chi-Square Tests into cohesive narratives contextualized with confidence intervals, sample sizes, and data limitations. Our evaluations comparing LLM performance to statisticians using the LLM-as-a-Judge framework suggest that AI can serve as a powerful descriptive assistant for real-time evidence generation when ethically incorporated in the analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [200] [SparkTales: Facilitating Cross-Language Collaborative Storytelling through Coordinator-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04806)
*Wenxin Zhao,Peng Zhang,Hansu Gu,Haoxuan Zhou,Xiaojie Huo,Lin Wang,Wen Zheng,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SparkTales是一个智能支持系统，旨在减轻跨语言协作故事讲述中协调者的认知负担，同时提升儿童的参与深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言协作故事讲述对儿童语言学习和文化发展至关重要，但实践中儿童参与度浅，协调者面临沉重的认知和组织负担，需要协调语言支持、维持儿童参与度并处理文化差异。

Method: 通过形成性研究了解协调者的需求和痛点，设计SparkTales智能支持系统，利用参与儿童的个体和共同特征，为协调者提供故事框架、多样化问题和理解导向的材料。

Result: 评估结果显示，SparkTales显著提高了协调者的指导效率和质量，同时改善了儿童的参与度，为未来支持跨语言协作的智能系统设计提供了宝贵见解。

Conclusion: SparkTales成功解决了跨语言协作故事讲述中的关键挑战，通过智能支持系统有效减轻协调者负担并提升儿童参与，为相关领域系统设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-language collaborative storytelling plays a vital role in children's language learning and cultural development, fostering both expressive ability and intercultural awareness. Yet, in practice, children's participation is often shallow, and facilitating such sessions places heavy cognitive and organizational burdens on coordinators, who must coordinate language support, maintain children's engagement, and navigate cultural differences. To address these challenges, we conducted a formative study with coordinators to identify their needs and pain points, which guided the design of SparkTales, an intelligent support system for cross-language collaborative storytelling. SparkTales leverages both individual and common characteristics of participating children to provide coordinators with story frameworks, diverse questions, and comprehension-oriented materials, aiming to reduce coordinators' workload while enhancing children's interactive engagement. Evaluation results show that SparkTales not only significantly increases coordinators' efficiency and quality of guidance but also improves children's participation, providing valuable insights for the design of future intelligent systems supporting cross-language collaboration.

</details>


### [201] [Roomify: Spatially-Grounded Style Transformation for Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04917)
*Xueyang Wang,Qinxuan Cen,Weitao Bi,Yunxiang Ma,Xin Yi,Robert Xiao,Xinyi Fu,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Roomify是一个空间锚定的虚拟环境生成系统，能够在保持物理房间空间结构和功能语义的同时，生成主题化的虚拟环境，解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VR方法面临基本权衡：完全沉浸会牺牲空间感知，而透视解决方案会破坏临场感。需要一种既能保持空间意识又能提供沉浸体验的方法。

Method: 采用空间锚定转换方法，将物理空间视为"空间容器"，保持家具的关键功能和几何属性，同时实现风格转换。结合现场3D场景理解、AI驱动的空间推理和风格感知生成，创建基于物理现实的个性化虚拟环境。提供跨现实创作工具，支持MR编辑和VR预览工作流。

Result: 用户研究显示：18名VR用户的临场感比透视方案提高63%，比完全虚拟基线提高26%，同时保持空间感知。8名设计专业人员确认系统创意表达性（场景质量：5.95/7；创意支持：6.08/7）和专业工作流价值。

Conclusion: Roomify通过空间锚定转换成功解决了VR中沉浸感与空间感知的权衡问题，在保持物理空间结构和功能语义的同时，实现了高度沉浸的虚拟环境生成，为跨现实创作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present Roomify, a spatially-grounded transformation system that generates themed virtual environments anchored to users' physical rooms while maintaining spatial structure and functional semantics. Current VR approaches face a fundamental trade-off: full immersion sacrifices spatial awareness, while passthrough solutions break presence. Roomify addresses this through spatially-grounded transformation - treating physical spaces as "spatial containers" that preserve key functional and geometric properties of furniture while enabling radical stylistic changes. Our pipeline combines in-situ 3D scene understanding, AI-driven spatial reasoning, and style-aware generation to create personalized virtual environments grounded in physical reality. We introduce a cross-reality authoring tool enabling fine-grained user control through MR editing and VR preview workflows. Two user studies validate our approach: one with 18 VR users demonstrates a 63% improvement in presence over passthrough and 26% over fully virtual baselines while maintaining spatial awareness; another with 8 design professionals confirms the system's creative expressiveness (scene quality: 5.95/7; creativity support: 6.08/7) and professional workflow value across diverse environments.

</details>


### [202] [Mind the Gap: Mapping Wearer-Bystander Privacy Tensions and Context-Adaptive Pathways for Camera Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04930)
*Xueyang Wang,Kewen Peng,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过调查和访谈系统评估了智能眼镜的隐私机制，发现旁观者与佩戴者之间存在期望差距，评估了12种隐私增强技术，揭示了四种基本权衡，并提出基于情境的自适应隐私保护路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜在佩戴者寻求记录功能与旁观者担心未经授权监控之间产生根本性隐私紧张关系，需要系统评估隐私机制以解决这一多利益相关方问题。

Method: 采用中国样本进行系统性多利益相关方评估，包括调查(N=525)和配对访谈(N=20)。研究1量化期望-意愿差距，研究2评估12种隐私增强技术。

Result: 发现旁观者始终要求比佩戴者愿意提供的更强的信息透明度和保护措施，在敏感情境下65-90%的旁观者会采取防御行动。评估的隐私技术揭示了四种基本权衡：可见性与干扰、赋权与负担、保护与自主权、问责与暴露。

Conclusion: 这些差距反映了结构性不相容而非善意不足，情境是隐私可接受性的主要决定因素。提出情境自适应路径：公共场所最小摩擦可见性、半公共环境结构化协商、敏感情境自动保护，为评估隐私机制和情境感知设计提供诊断框架。

Abstract: Camera glasses create fundamental privacy tensions between wearers seeking recording functionality and bystanders concerned about unauthorized surveillance. We present a systematic multi-stakeholder evaluation of privacy mechanisms through surveys (N=525) and paired interviews (N=20) in China. Study 1 quantifies expectation-willingness gaps: bystanders consistently demand stronger information transparency and protective measures than wearers will provide, with disparities intensifying in sensitive contexts where 65-90% of bystanders would take defensive action. Study 2 evaluates twelve privacy-enhancing technologies, revealing four fundamental trade-offs that undermine current approaches: visibility versus disruption, empowerment versus burden, protection versus agency, and accountability versus exposure. These gaps reflect structural incompatibilities rather than inadequate goodwill, with context emerging as the primary determinant of privacy acceptability. We propose context-adaptive pathways that dynamically adjust protection strategies: minimal-friction visibility in public spaces, structured negotiation in semi-public environments, and automatic protection in sensitive contexts. Our findings contribute a diagnostic framework for evaluating privacy mechanisms and implications for context-aware design in ubiquitous sensing.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Advocacy: A Design Space for Replication-Related Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04959)
*Yiheng Liang,Kim Marriott,Helen C. Purchase*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于复制研究设计的四维度三层次设计空间框架，支持复制实验的识别、分类、比较和分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制研究在科学领域（包括可视化和人机交互）中非常重要，但在进行复制研究时需要决定哪些方面保持不变、哪些需要改变，缺乏系统化的设计决策框架

Method: 将复制实验设计视为成对比较问题，提出一个四维度三层次的设计空间框架，用于识别、分类、比较和分析复制研究的设计决策

Result: 开发了一个实用的设计空间框架，可用于回顾性特征描述和前瞻性规划，提供了工作示例，并将该框架与其他描述复制研究范围的尝试联系起来

Conclusion: 提出的多维度设计空间框架为复制研究提供了系统化的设计决策支持工具，有助于更好地理解和规划复制实验

Abstract: The importance of replication is often discussed and advocated -- not only in the domains of visualization and HCI, but in all scientific areas. When replicating a study, design decisions need to be made with regards which aspects of the original study will remain the same and which will be altered. We present a supporting multi-dimensional design space framework within which such decisions can be identified, categorized, compared and analyzed. The framework treats replication experimental design as a pairwise comparison problem, and represents the design by four practical dimensions defined by three comparison levels. The design space is therefore a framework that can be used for both retrospective characterization and prospective planning. We provide worked examples, and relate our framework to other attempts at describing the scope of replication studies.

</details>


### [204] [Auto-Generating Personas from User Reviews in VR App Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04985)
*Yi Wang,Kexin Cheng,Xiao Liu,Chetan Arora,John Grundy,Thuong Hoang,Henry Been-Lirn Duh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了VR课程中的自动生成人物角色系统，用于促进无障碍需求讨论和指导VR设计开发


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人物角色是讨论软件设计中无障碍需求的有价值工具，但在VR项目中用于无障碍需求获取的应用有限且面临挑战

Method: 在VR课程中开发自动生成人物角色系统，使用这些角色促进无障碍需求讨论并指导VR设计和开发

Result: 自动生成人物角色系统使学生能够更有效地培养同理心

Conclusion: 本研究展示了在VR课程环境中使用自动生成人物角色作为获取潜在无障碍需求的方法

Abstract: Personas are a valuable tool for discussing accessibility requirements in software design and development practices. However, the use of personas for accessibility-focused requirements elicitation in VR projects remains limited and is accompanied by several challenges. To fill this gap, we developed an auto-generated persona system in a VR course, where the personas were used to facilitate discussions on accessibility requirements and to guide VR design and development. Our findings indicate that the auto-generated persona system enabled students to develop empathy more efficiently. This study demonstrates the use of automatically generated personas in VR course settings as a means of eliciting latent accessibility requirements.

</details>


### [205] [Haptics in Cognition: Disruptor or Enabler of Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05019)
*Bibeg Limbu,Irene-Angelica Chounta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索性试点研究调查触觉感知（触觉敏感性和动觉强度）对学习的影响，通过手写测量信息保留。研究发现增加书写压力可能轻微损害即时回忆，而单独使用手套无明显影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索触觉感知（特别是触觉敏感性和动觉强度）如何影响学习过程，特别是信息保留。这有助于理解具身交互与认知结果之间的复杂关系，为设计基于传感器的多模态学习环境提供依据。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计的探索性试点研究，随机分配20名参与者到四个实验组，操纵触觉（通过使用手套）和运动（通过增加书写压力）。测量信息保留（即时回忆测试）、心理努力（次要任务反应时间）和感知工作量（NASA-TLX）。使用贝叶斯二项回归和贝叶斯中介分析进行数据分析。

Result: 贝叶斯二项回归显示中等证据表明增加书写压力对回忆有负面影响（85-88%概率为负效应），而单独使用手套无明显影响。贝叶斯中介分析未发现心理努力或感知工作量中介这些效应的强证据，所有95%可信区间包含零，表明存在显著不确定性。

Conclusion: 增加动觉需求可能轻微损害即时回忆，且独立于感知工作量或心理努力。单独操纵触觉不影响信息保留。研究为理解具身交互与认知结果之间的细微关系做出贡献，对设计传感器多模态学习环境有启示意义。

Abstract: This exploratory pilot study investigates the impact of haptic perception --specifically tactile sensitivity (touch) and kinaesthetic intensity (movement)-- on learning, operationalized as information retention (immediate recall) through handwriting. Participants (N=20) were randomly assigned to one of four experimental groups in a 2x2 factorial design, manipulating touch (via glove use) and movement (via increased writing pressure). Information retention was measured using an immediate recall test, while mental effort (reaction time in a secondary task) and perceived workload (NASA-TLX) were examined as mediating variables. Bayesian binomial regression revealed moderate evidence that increased writing pressure negatively influenced recall (85-88% probability of negative effect), whereas glove use alone demonstrated no clear effect. Bayesian mediation analysis found no strong evidence that mental effort or perceived workload mediated these effects, as all 95% credible intervals included zero, indicating substantial uncertainty. These findings suggest that increased Kinaesthetic demands may slightly impair immediate recall, independent of perceived workload or mental effort. Importantly, the manipulation of touch alone does not appear to influence information retention. The study contributes to understanding the nuanced relationship between embodied interactions and cognitive outcomes, with implications for designing sensor-based multimodal learning environments.

</details>


### [206] [Wire Your Way: Hardware-Contextualized Guidance and In-situ Tests for Personalized Circuit Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05085)
*Punn Lertjaturaphat,Jungwoo Rhee,Jaewon You,Andrea Bianchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发支持个性化电路构建和调试的原型平台，通过增强型面包板提供电路感知、实时硬件重新配置和现场电路验证功能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教程和基于教程的系统采用逐步指导方式，与创客独特的电路构建偏好和问题评估调试方式不匹配，需要支持个性化电路构建和调试的平台

Method: 开发电路感知的增强型面包板，支持通过情境化指导实现实时硬件重新配置，并通过交互式测试进行现场电路验证

Result: 通过可用性研究（N=12）证明创客能够利用电路感知指导和调试功能来支持个体化的构建模式

Conclusion: 提出的原型平台通过增强型面包板有效支持创客的个性化电路构建和调试需求，解决了传统教程方法与创客实际工作方式不匹配的问题

Abstract: The increasing popularity of microcontroller platforms like Arduino enables diverse end-user developers to participate in circuit prototyping. Traditionally, follow-along tutorials serve as an essential learning method for makers, and in fact, several prior toolkits leveraged this format as a way to engage new makers. However, literature and our formative study (N=12) show that makers have unique preferences regarding the construction of their circuits and idiosyncratic ways to assess and debug problems, which contrasts with the step-by-step instructional nature of tutorials and those systems leveraging this method. To address this mismatch, we present a prototyping platform that supports personalized circuit construction and debugging. Our system utilizes an augmented breadboard, which is circuit-aware and supports on-the-fly hardware reconfiguration via contextualized guidance and in-situ circuit validation through interactive tests. Through a usability study (N=12), we demonstrate how makers leverage circuit-aware guidance and debugging to support individual building patterns.

</details>


### [207] [Not All Trust is the Same: Effects of Decision Workflow and Explanations in Human-AI Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05229)
*Laura Spillner,Rachel Ringe,Robert Porzel,Rainer Malaka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了AI辅助决策中两种工作流程（一步式vs两步式）对信任校准的影响，发现两步式设置并未减少过度依赖，且工作流程与解释、领域知识存在交互效应，证实了报告信任与依赖行为是不同的构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助决策中需要实现合理、校准的信任，避免过度信任（接受错误建议）和信任不足（拒绝正确建议）。先前研究在决策工作流程设计（一步式vs两步式）和信任测量方法（自我报告vs行为依赖）上存在差异，需要系统研究这些因素及其交互作用。

Method: 研究考察了(a)决策工作流程类型（一步式vs两步式）、(b)解释的存在、(c)用户领域知识和AI经验对信任的影响。比较了报告信任、依赖行为（同意率和切换率）以及过度依赖。通过实验设计分析这些因素的交互效应。

Result: 结果显示两步式设置并未减少过度依赖；决策工作流程不直接影响自我报告信任，但与领域知识和解释存在交叉交互效应，表明解释的效果可能因工作流程设置而异；报告信任与依赖行为是不同的构念，需要分别评估。

Conclusion: AI辅助决策中，两步式工作流程不能有效减少过度依赖；解释的效果受工作流程设置影响；报告信任与行为依赖是独立构念，应分别测量；未来研究需考虑工作流程设计与解释的交互作用。

Abstract: A central challenge in AI-assisted decision making is achieving warranted, well-calibrated trust. Both overtrust (accepting incorrect AI recommendations) and undertrust (rejecting correct advice) should be prevented. Prior studies differ in the design of the decision workflow - whether users see the AI suggestion immediately (1-step setup) or have to submit a first decision beforehand (2-step setup) -, and in how trust is measured - through self-reports or as behavioral trust, that is, reliance. We examined the effects and interactions of (a) the type of decision workflow, (b) the presence of explanations, and (c) users' domain knowledge and prior AI experience. We compared reported trust, reliance (agreement rate and switch rate), and overreliance. Results showed no evidence that a 2-step setup reduces overreliance. The decision workflow also did not directly affect self-reported trust, but there was a crossover interaction effect with domain knowledge and explanations, suggesting that the effects of explanations alone may not generalize across workflow setups. Finally, our findings confirm that reported trust and reliance behavior are distinct constructs that should be evaluated separately in AI-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [208] [AttentiveLearn: Personalized Post-Lecture Support for Gaze-Aware Immersive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05324)
*Shi Liu,Martin Feick,Linus Bierhoff,Alexander Maedche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AttentiveLearn：基于VR眼动追踪的个性化课后测验系统，通过分析学习者在VR课堂中的注意力分布，在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验，提升学习动机和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式学习环境（如VR虚拟教室）为学习者提供独特体验，但有效学习支持仍具挑战。现有HCI研究主要关注课中支持，而课后支持对维持学习动机、参与度和学习成果至关重要，却鲜有研究。

Method: 开发AttentiveLearn学习生态系统，利用VR课堂中的眼动追踪推断学习者注意力分布，基于此在移动学习助手上生成个性化测验。通过为期四周的实地研究，招募36名大学生参与贝叶斯数据分析课程，评估系统效果。

Result: AttentiveLearn显著提升了学习者的报告动机和参与度，但学习成效的证据尚不明确。同时，轶事证据表明部分参与者的注意力随时间有所改善。

Conclusion: 研究为沉浸式学习系统提供了个性化课后支持的实证见解和设计启示，强调课后支持对维持学习动机和参与度的重要性，为未来沉浸式学习支持系统设计提供参考。

Abstract: Immersive learning environments such as virtual classrooms in Virtual Reality (VR) offer learners unique learning experiences, yet providing effective learner support remains a challenge. While prior HCI research has explored in-lecture support for immersive learning, little research has been conducted to provide post-lecture support, despite being critical for sustained motivation, engagement, and learning outcomes. To address this, we present AttentiveLearn, a learning ecosystem that generates personalized quizzes on a mobile learning assistant based on learners' attention distribution inferred using eye-tracking in VR lectures. We evaluated the system in a four-week field study with 36 university students attending lectures on Bayesian data analysis. AttentiveLearn improved learners' reported motivation and engagement, without conclusive evidence of learning gains. Meanwhile, anecdotal evidence suggested improvements in attention for certain participants over time. Based on our findings of the field study, we provide empirical insights and design implications for personalized post-lecture support for immersive learning systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy for Causal Direction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04473)
*Harikrishnan N B,Shubham Bhilare,Aditi Kathpalia,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于字典的模式熵（DPE）框架，利用算法信息论和香农信息论从时序符号序列中发现因果方向，通过最小不确定性准则识别因果方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号序列的因果方向发现具有挑战性，因为缺乏功能模型和噪声假设。需要一种能够从时序观测数据中推断因果方向并识别驱动变化的特定子模式的方法。

Method: 提出字典模式熵（DPE）框架，将因果关系解释为候选原因中紧凑、基于规则的模式的涌现，这些模式系统地约束效应。构建方向特定的字典，使用基于熵的度量量化其影响，建立确定性模式结构和随机变异性之间的原则性联系。

Result: DPE在多种合成系统中（延迟比特翻转扰动、AR(1)耦合、1D斜帐篷映射、稀疏过程）始终实现可靠性能，优于或匹配竞争性AIT方法（ETC_E、ETC_P、LZ_P）。在生物和生态数据集中表现具有竞争力，而替代方法在特定基因组设置中显示出优势。

Conclusion: 最小化模式级不确定性产生了一个稳健、可解释且广泛适用的因果发现框架。DPE通过整合算法信息论和香农信息论，为符号序列的因果推断提供了新的理论和方法基础。

Abstract: Discovering causal direction from temporal observational data is particularly challenging for symbolic sequences, where functional models and noise assumptions are often unavailable. We propose a novel \emph{Dictionary Based Pattern Entropy ($DPE$)} framework that infers both the direction of causation and the specific subpatterns driving changes in the effect variable. The framework integrates \emph{Algorithmic Information Theory} (AIT) and \emph{Shannon Information Theory}. Causation is interpreted as the emergence of compact, rule based patterns in the candidate cause that systematically constrain the effect. $DPE$ constructs direction-specific dictionaries and quantifies their influence using entropy-based measures, enabling a principled link between deterministic pattern structure and stochastic variability. Causal direction is inferred via a minimum-uncertainty criterion, selecting the direction exhibiting stronger and more consistent pattern-driven organization. As summarized in Table 7, $DPE$ consistently achieves reliable performance across diverse synthetic systems, including delayed bit-flip perturbations, AR(1) coupling, 1D skew-tent maps, and sparse processes, outperforming or matching competing AIT-based methods ($ETC_E$, $ETC_P$, $LZ_P$). In biological and ecological datasets, performance is competitive, while alternative methods show advantages in specific genomic settings. Overall, the results demonstrate that minimizing pattern level uncertainty yields a robust, interpretable, and broadly applicable framework for causal discovery.

</details>


### [210] [Bayesian Modeling of Collatz Stopping Times: A Probabilistic Machine Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04479)
*Nicolò Bonacorsi,Matteo Bordoni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 使用贝叶斯负二项回归和奇块分解生成模型分析Collatz总停止时间的统计特性，发现低阶模结构是异质性的关键驱动因素


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从概率机器学习视角研究Collatz总停止时间τ(n)的统计特性，该函数表现出偏斜、过度离散和算术异质性，需要开发统计模型来理解和预测其行为

Method: 1. 贝叶斯分层负二项回归(NB2-GLM)：使用log n和n mod 8作为协变量预测τ(n)，通过后验和后验预测分布量化不确定性
2. 基于奇块分解的机制生成近似：对奇数m，将3m+1分解为2^{K(m)}m'，随机化块长度并通过Dirichlet-multinomial更新校准

Result: 在保留数据上，NB2-GLM比奇块生成器获得显著更高的预测似然；将块长度分布条件化于m mod 8可显著改善生成器的分布拟合，表明低阶模结构是τ(n)异质性的关键驱动因素

Conclusion: Collatz总停止时间具有显著的算术异质性，低阶模结构是理解其统计行为的关键；贝叶斯回归模型在预测性能上优于机制生成模型，但条件化于模结构的生成器能更好地捕捉分布特征

Abstract: We study the Collatz total stopping time $τ(n)$ over $n\le 10^7$ from a probabilistic machine learning viewpoint. Empirically, $τ(n)$ is a skewed and heavily overdispersed count with pronounced arithmetic heterogeneity. We develop two complementary models. First, a Bayesian hierarchical Negative Binomial regression (NB2-GLM) predicts $τ(n)$ from simple covariates ($\log n$ and residue class $n \bmod 8$), quantifying uncertainty via posterior and posterior predictive distributions. Second, we propose a mechanistic generative approximation based on the odd-block decomposition: for odd $m$, write $3m+1=2^{K(m)}m'$ with $m'$ odd and $K(m)=v_2(3m+1)\ge 1$; randomizing these block lengths yields a stochastic approximation calibrated via a Dirichlet-multinomial update. On held-out data, the NB2-GLM achieves substantially higher predictive likelihood than the odd-block generators. Conditioning the block-length distribution on $m\bmod 8$ markedly improves the generator's distributional fit, indicating that low-order modular structure is a key driver of heterogeneity in $τ(n)$.

</details>


### [211] [The Volterra signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04525)
*Paul P. Hager,Fabian N. Harang,Luca Pelizzari,Samy Tindel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Volterra签名作为历史依赖系统的显式特征表示，通过张量代数中的时间核加权路径，提供可解释性、理论保证和计算可行性，在动态学习任务中优于经典路径签名基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非马尔可夫时间序列学习方法（如RNN、神经控制微分方程、Transformer）依赖隐式记忆机制，存在可解释性差、长时程训练困难的问题，需要一种原则性的显式特征表示方法。

Method: 提出Volterra签名VSig(x;K)，通过时间核K对输入路径x进行加权，将其展开到张量代数中，利用Volterra-Chen恒等式建立理论保证，包括可识别性和通用逼近定理，并展示核技巧的适用性。

Result: 证明了Volterra签名的可识别性（在增强条件下的可辨识性）和无限维路径空间上的通用逼近定理；对于指数型核，Volterra签名可表示为张量代数中的线性状态空间ODE；在真实和合成数据的动态学习任务中，该方法持续改进经典路径签名基线。

Conclusion: Volterra签名作为一种稳健、计算可行的特征映射，具有时间重参数化不变性，为历史依赖系统提供了原则性的显式特征表示，在理论和实际应用中均表现出优越性。

Abstract: Modern approaches for learning from non-Markovian time series, such as recurrent neural networks, neural controlled differential equations or transformers, typically rely on implicit memory mechanisms that can be difficult to interpret or to train over long horizons. We propose the Volterra signature $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ as a principled, explicit feature representation for history-dependent systems. By developing the input path $x$ weighted by a temporal kernel $K$ into the tensor algebra, we leverage the associated Volterra--Chen identity to derive rigorous learning-theoretic guarantees. Specifically, we prove an injectivity statement (identifiability under augmentation) that leads to a universal approximation theorem on the infinite dimensional path space, which in certain cases is achieved by linear functionals of $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$. Moreover, we demonstrate applicability of the kernel trick by showing that the inner product associated with Volterra signatures admits a closed characterization via a two-parameter integral equation, enabling numerical methods from PDEs for computation. For a large class of exponential-type kernels, $\mathrm{VSig}(x;K)$ solves a linear state-space ODE in the tensor algebra. Combined with inherent invariance to time reparameterization, these results position the Volterra signature as a robust, computationally tractable feature map for data science. We demonstrate its efficacy in dynamic learning tasks on real and synthetic data, where it consistently improves classical path signature baselines.

</details>


### [212] [Optimal Prediction-Augmented Algorithms for Testing Independence of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04635)
*Maryam Aliakbarpour,Alireza Azizi,Ria Stevens*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增强分布测试框架的独立性检验方法，通过整合辅助预测信息来提高样本效率，同时保持最坏情况下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非参数有限样本独立性检验存在样本复杂度高的问题，需要随支持集大小多项式增长。为了超越这些最坏情况限制，研究者希望利用预测信息来提高检验效率。

Method: 采用增强分布测试框架，设计整合辅助预测信息的独立性检验器。该方法保持最坏情况下的有效性，同时在预测准确时显著提高样本效率。具体包括：1) 针对离散分布的二元独立性检验器，能根据预测误差自适应降低样本复杂度；2) 扩展到高维多变量设置，用于检验d个随机变量的独立性。

Result: 提出了三种主要贡献：1) 二元独立性检验器能自适应调整样本复杂度；2) 扩展到高维多变量独立性检验；3) 匹配的最小最大下界证明这些检验器达到了最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过整合预测信息，该框架在保持最坏情况鲁棒性的同时，显著提高了独立性检验的样本效率，为统计推断中的基本问题提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Independence testing is a fundamental problem in statistical inference: given samples from a joint distribution $p$ over multiple random variables, the goal is to determine whether $p$ is a product distribution or is $ε$-far from all product distributions in total variation distance. In the non-parametric finite-sample regime, this task is notoriously expensive, as the minimax sample complexity scales polynomially with the support size. In this work, we move beyond these worst-case limitations by leveraging the framework of \textit{augmented distribution testing}. We design independence testers that incorporate auxiliary, but potentially untrustworthy, predictive information. Our framework ensures that the tester remains robust, maintaining worst-case validity regardless of the prediction's quality, while significantly improving sample efficiency when the prediction is accurate. Our main contributions include: (i) a bivariate independence tester for discrete distributions that adaptively reduces sample complexity based on the prediction error; (ii) a generalization to the high-dimensional multivariate setting for testing the independence of $d$ random variables; and (iii) matching minimax lower bounds demonstrating that our testers achieve optimal sample complexity.

</details>


### [213] [Learning Optimal Individualized Decision Rules with Conditional Demographic Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05226)
*Wenhai Cui,Wen Su,Donglin Zeng,Xingqiu Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种在个体化决策规则中融入人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束的新框架，通过扰动无约束最优决策规则实现高效计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化决策规则在个性化营销、医疗和公共政策中广泛应用，但基于有偏数据训练的算法可能对性别、种族等敏感属性定义的少数群体造成歧视性伤害，需要解决这一伦理问题

Method: 提出将人口统计均等和条件人口统计均等约束纳入最优个体化决策规则估计的新框架，通过向无约束最优决策规则施加扰动来获得理论最优约束决策规则，实现计算高效解

Result: 理论上推导了策略价值和公平约束项的收敛率，通过综合模拟研究和俄勒冈健康保险实验的实证应用验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该框架为个体化决策规则中的公平性问题提供了理论保证和计算高效的解决方案，能够有效减少算法对少数群体的歧视性影响

Abstract: Individualized decision rules (IDRs) have become increasingly prevalent in societal applications such as personalized marketing, healthcare, and public policy design. However, a critical ethical concern arises from the potential discriminatory effects of IDRs trained on biased data. These algorithms may disproportionately harm individuals from minority subgroups defined by sensitive attributes like gender, race, or language. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that incorporates demographic parity (DP) and conditional demographic parity (CDP) constraints into the estimation of optimal IDRs. We show that the theoretically optimal IDRs under DP and CDP constraints can be obtained by applying perturbations to the unconstrained optimal IDRs, enabling a computationally efficient solution. Theoretically, we derive convergence rates for both policy value and the fairness constraint term. The effectiveness of our methods is illustrated through comprehensive simulation studies and an empirical application to the Oregon Health Insurance Experiment.

</details>


### [214] [Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05288)
*Luwei Wang,Nazir Lone,Sohan Seth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类（BSCC）方法，以处理效应为结果指导聚类过程，识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗和政策评估等领域，识别具有相似特征的亚组对于个性化决策至关重要。现有方法多依赖无监督聚类，但监督聚类方法能针对特定关注结果识别可操作的亚组

Method: 提出贝叶斯监督因果聚类（BSCC）框架，以处理效应作为结果指导聚类过程，识别协变量特征和处理效应均相似的个体亚组

Result: 在模拟数据集和真实世界数据集（第三次国际卒中试验）上评估BSCC，验证了该框架的实际应用价值

Conclusion: BSCC为识别具有相似处理效应的同质亚组提供了一种有效的监督因果聚类方法，在个性化决策中具有重要应用价值

Abstract: Finding patient subgroups with similar characteristics is crucial for personalized decision-making in various disciplines such as healthcare and policy evaluation. While most existing approaches rely on unsupervised clustering methods, there is a growing trend toward using supervised clustering methods that identify operationalizable subgroups in the context of a specific outcome of interest. We propose Bayesian Supervised Causal Clustering (BSCC), with treatment effect as outcome to guide the clustering process. BSCC identifies homogenous subgroups of individuals who are similar in their covariate profiles as well as their treatment effects. We evaluate BSCC on simulated datasets as well as real-world dataset from the third International Stroke Trial to assess the practical usefulness of the framework.

</details>


### [215] [Bayes with No Shame: Admissibility Geometries of Predictive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05335)
*Nicholas G. Polson,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文证明了四种不同的可容许性几何在序列和无分布推断中互不嵌套，每种几何都有不同的最优性证明方法，且可容许性是准则相关的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解序列和无分布推断中四种不同可容许性几何之间的关系，揭示它们在几何上的不兼容性，证明可容许性依赖于具体准则而非绝对概念。

Method: 通过准则分离定理证明四种可容许性类别两两不嵌套，分析每种几何的最优性证明方法（支持超平面先验、非负上鞅、可交换性秩、Cesàro引导论证），比较鞅相干性在不同准则中的作用。

Result: 证明了四种可容许性几何是两两不嵌套的，每种几何都有独特的最优性证明方法，鞅相干性对Blackwell可容许性是必要但非充分的，对随时有效可容许性是必要且充分的，但对覆盖有效性和CAA可容许性既非必要也非充分。

Conclusion: 可容许性是准则相关的，四种几何虽然共享共同的优化模板，但约束集在不同空间、偏序和性能度量上操作，导致几何上的不兼容性，不存在统一的可容许性概念。

Abstract: Four distinct admissibility geometries govern sequential and distribution-free inference: Blackwell risk dominance over convex risk sets, anytime-valid admissibility within the nonnegative supermartingale cone, marginal coverage validity over exchangeable prediction sets, and Cesàro approachability (CAA) admissibility, which reaches the risk-set boundary via approachability-style arguments rather than explicit priors. We prove a criterion separation theorem: the four classes of admissible procedures are pairwise non-nested. Each geometry carries a different certificate of optimality: a supporting-hyperplane prior (Blackwell), a nonnegative supermartingale (anytime-valid), an exchangeability rank (coverage), or a Cesàro steering argument (CAA). Martingale coherence is necessary for Blackwell admissibility and necessary and sufficient for anytime-valid admissibility within e-processes, but is not sufficient for Blackwell admissibility and is not necessary for coverage validity or CAA-admissibility. All four criteria share a common optimization template (minimize Bayesian risk subject to a feasibility constraint), but the constraint sets operate over different spaces, partial orders, and performance metrics, making them geometrically incompatible. Admissibility is irreducibly criterion-relative.

</details>


### [216] [How important are the genes to explain the outcome - the asymmetric Shapley value as an honest importance metric for high-dimensional features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05317)
*Mark A. van de Wiel,Jeroen Goedhart,Martin Jullum,Kjersti Aas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用非对称Shapley值来量化混合维度预测模型中的特征重要性，特别针对临床预测中基因组学特征与传统临床变量的重要性评估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床预测中，评估高维特征（如基因组学）重要性时，传统方法通常将其添加到传统临床变量集中并评估预测性能变化。这种方法存在问题，因为它既没有考虑变量间的共线性，也没有考虑已知的依赖方向性。

Method: 提出使用非对称Shapley值作为更合适的替代方法来量化混合维度预测模型中的特征重要性。特别关注临床预测中特别相关的设置：疾病状态作为基因组效应的中介变量，以及可能存在未知效应方向的额外混杂因素。推导了计算该设置下局部和全局非对称Shapley值的高效算法。

Result: 局部非对称Shapley值被证明对推断非常有用，而全局非对称Shapley值通过将任何预测性能指标分解为特征的贡献来提供解释。整个框架通过结直肠癌患者无进展生存期预测的示例进行说明。

Conclusion: 非对称Shapley值是量化混合维度预测模型中特征重要性的更合适方法，能够解决传统方法在考虑共线性、依赖方向性和临床预测特定设置方面的局限性。

Abstract: In clinical prediction settings the importance of a high-dimensional feature like genomics is often assessed by evaluating the change in predictive performance when adding it to a set of traditional clinical variables. This approach is questionable, because it does not account for collinearity nor known directionality of dependencies between variables. We suggest to use asymmetric Shapley values as a more suitable alternative to quantify feature importance in the context of a mixed-dimensional prediction model. We focus on a setting that is particularly relevant in clinical prediction: disease state as a mediating variable for genomic effects, with additional confounders for which the direction of effects may be unknown. We derive efficient algorithms to compute local and global asymmetric Shapley values for this setting. The former are shown to be very useful for inference, whereas the latter provide interpretation by decomposing any predictive performance metric into contributions of the features. Throughout, we illustrate our framework by a leading example: the prediction of progression-free survival for colorectal cancer patients.

</details>


### [217] [On the Statistical Optimality of Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05340)
*Zineng Xu,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Yan Shuo Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为经验风险最小化（ERM）决策树建立了全面的统计理论，包括高维回归和分类中的尖锐oracle不等式、最小化最优速率，并引入了适用于重尾噪声的稳健保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然全局最优ERM决策树在计算上已可行且经验上成功，但其统计性能的严格理论保证仍然有限。需要为ERM树建立全面的统计理论，特别是在高维随机设计设置下。

Method: 使用基于经验局部化Rademacher复杂度的新型均匀集中框架，建立尖锐的oracle不等式。推导了在新型函数类——分段稀疏异质各向异性Besov（PSHAB）空间上的最小化最优速率。该方法在亚高斯假设下建立主要结果，同时提供适用于重尾噪声设置的稳健保证。

Result: 建立了ERM树相对于最多L个叶子的最佳可能逼近的过剩风险界限，刻画了可解释性-准确性权衡。推导了在PSHAB空间上的最小化最优速率，该空间明确捕捉了实践中遇到的三个关键结构特征：稀疏性、各向异性平滑性和空间异质性。

Conclusion: 该研究为ERM树的最优性提供了原则性基础，并引入了经验过程工具，这些工具广泛适用于其他高度自适应、数据驱动的程序。这些发现填补了ERM决策树理论保证的空白。

Abstract: While globally optimal empirical risk minimization (ERM) decision trees have become computationally feasible and empirically successful, rigorous theoretical guarantees for their statistical performance remain limited. In this work, we develop a comprehensive statistical theory for ERM trees under random design in both high-dimensional regression and classification. We first establish sharp oracle inequalities that bound the excess risk of the ERM estimator relative to the best possible approximation achievable by any tree with at most $L$ leaves, thereby characterizing the interpretability-accuracy trade-off. We derive these results using a novel uniform concentration framework based on empirically localized Rademacher complexity. Furthermore, we derive minimax optimal rates over a novel function class: the piecewise sparse heterogeneous anisotropic Besov (PSHAB) space. This space explicitly captures three key structural features encountered in practice: sparsity, anisotropic smoothness, and spatial heterogeneity. While our main results are established under sub-Gaussianity, we also provide robust guarantees that hold under heavy-tailed noise settings. Together, these findings provide a principled foundation for the optimality of ERM trees and introduce empirical process tools broadly applicable to other highly adaptive, data-driven procedures.

</details>


### [218] [Harnessing Synthetic Data from Generative AI for Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05396)
*Ahmad Abdel-Azim,Ruoyu Wang,Xihong Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文从统计学视角系统综述了生成式AI模型产生的合成数据的现状，分析了合成数据在科学发现、推断和预测中的有效使用条件，指出了常见陷阱，并提出了原则性使用框架和实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的快速发展极大地增加了合成数据的可用性和应用范围，但同时也引发了关于合成数据何时能够以有效、可靠和原则性方式使用的根本性统计问题。论文旨在澄清合成数据能够有意义支持下游发现、推断和预测的假设条件。

Method: 论文采用综述研究方法：1) 调查现代生成模型的主要类别及其预期用例和优势；2) 分析合成数据作为真实观测替代品时的常见陷阱，包括模型误设偏差、不确定性衰减和泛化困难；3) 基于这些见解讨论合成数据的原则性使用框架。

Result: 论文系统梳理了生成模型的局限性特征和失败模式，识别了合成数据使用中的关键问题，包括模型误设导致的偏差、不确定性估计不足以及泛化能力受限等。提出了新兴的原则性使用框架来应对这些挑战。

Conclusion: 论文为方法开发者和应用研究者提供了实用建议、开放问题和注意事项，旨在指导合成数据的负责任使用。强调需要明确合成数据使用的统计假设，避免将合成数据简单视为真实观测的替代品，并建立原则性框架来确保合成数据在科学发现、推断和预测中的有效性。

Abstract: The emergence of generative AI models has dramatically expanded the availability and use of synthetic data across scientific, industrial, and policy domains. While these developments open new possibilities for data analysis, they also raise fundamental statistical questions about when synthetic data can be used in a valid, reliable, and principled manner. This paper reviews the current landscape of synthetic data generation and use from a statistical perspective, with the goal of clarifying the assumptions under which synthetic data can meaningfully support downstream discovery, inference, and prediction. We survey major classes of modern generative models, their intended use cases, and the benefits they offer, while also highlighting their limitations and characteristic failure modes. We additionally examine common pitfalls that arise when synthetic data are treated as surrogates for real observations, including biases from model misspecification, attenuated uncertainty, and difficulties in generalization. Building on these insights, we discuss emerging frameworks for the principled use of synthetic data. We conclude with practical recommendations, open problems, and cautions intended to guide both method developers and applied researchers.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Decorrelating the Future: Joint Frequency Domain Learning for Spatio-temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04418)
*Zepu Wang,Bowen Liao,Jeff,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreST Loss，一种频率增强的时空训练目标，通过联合傅里叶变换将监督扩展到联合时空频谱，有效解耦时空依赖关系，提升图结构信号预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准直接预测模型通常依赖点状目标（如均方误差），无法捕捉图结构信号中复杂的时空依赖关系。现有频率域方法（如FreDF）虽然缓解了时间自相关，但往往忽略了空间和跨时空交互作用。

Method: 提出FreST Loss，一种频率增强的时空训练目标，利用联合傅里叶变换（JFT）将模型预测与真实值在统一的频谱域中对齐，有效解耦空间和时间上的复杂依赖关系。

Result: 理论分析表明该公式减少了与时间域训练目标相关的估计偏差。在六个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明，FreST Loss是模型无关的，通过更好地捕捉整体时空动态，持续改进了最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FreST Loss通过将监督扩展到联合时空频谱，有效解决了现有方法在捕捉复杂时空依赖关系方面的局限性，为图结构信号预测提供了更全面的频率域训练框架。

Abstract: Standard direct forecasting models typically rely on point-wise objectives such as Mean Squared Error, which fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in graph-structured signals. While recent frequency-domain approaches such as FreDF mitigate temporal autocorrelation, they often overlook spatial and cross spatio-temporal interactions. To address this limitation, we propose FreST Loss, a frequency-enhanced spatio-temporal training objective that extends supervision to the joint spatio-temporal spectrum. By leveraging the Joint Fourier Transform (JFT), FreST Loss aligns model predictions with ground truth in a unified spectral domain, effectively decorrelating complex dependencies across both space and time. Theoretical analysis shows that this formulation reduces estimation bias associated with time-domain training objectives. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that FreST Loss is model-agnostic and consistently improves state-of-the-art baselines by better capturing holistic spatio-temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [220] [Delta-Crosscoder: Robust Crosscoder Model Diffing in Narrow Fine-Tuning Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04426)
*Aly Kassem,Thomas Jiralerspong,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delta-Crosscoder改进了模型差异分析方法，通过BatchTopK稀疏性和基于delta的损失函数，结合配对激活的隐式对比信号，有效识别微调导致的局部化、非对称行为变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Crosscoders方法在处理窄微调（行为变化局部化且非对称）时存在困难，需要更有效的模型差异分析工具来识别微调对模型内部表示的改变。

Method: Delta-Crosscoder结合BatchTopK稀疏性和基于delta的损失函数，优先关注模型间变化的方向，同时利用匹配输入上的配对激活提供隐式对比信号。

Result: 在10个模型（包括合成虚假事实、涌现错位、潜意识学习和禁忌词猜测等任务，涵盖Gemma、LLaMA、Qwen等1B-9B参数模型）上，Delta-Crosscoder可靠地分离出导致微调行为的潜在方向，并实现有效缓解，优于基于SAE的基线方法，与非SAE方法性能相当。

Conclusion: Delta-Crosscoder证明crosscoders仍然是模型差异分析的有力工具，能够有效处理窄微调场景，识别微调行为变化的因果方向。

Abstract: Model diffing methods aim to identify how fine-tuning changes a model's internal representations. Crosscoders approach this by learning shared dictionaries of interpretable latent directions between base and fine-tuned models. However, existing formulations struggle with narrow fine-tuning, where behavioral changes are localized and asymmetric. We introduce Delta-Crosscoder, which combines BatchTopK sparsity with a delta-based loss prioritizing directions that change between models, plus an implicit contrastive signal from paired activations on matched inputs. Evaluated across 10 model organisms, including synthetic false facts, emergent misalignment, subliminal learning, and taboo word guessing (Gemma, LLaMA, Qwen; 1B-9B parameters), Delta-Crosscoder reliably isolates latent directions causally responsible for fine-tuned behaviors and enables effective mitigation, outperforming SAE-based baselines, while matching the Non-SAE-based. Our results demonstrate that crosscoders remain a powerful tool for model diffing.

</details>


### [221] [Thin Keys, Full Values: Reducing KV Cache via Low-Dimensional Attention Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04427)
*Hengshuai Yao,Guan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出非对称注意力机制，认为查询和键用于选择（低维操作），而值用于语义传递（高维操作），通过降低QK维度减少参数和KV缓存，实现显著的内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer注意力中查询、键和值使用相同维度，但作者认为这三者功能不同：查询和键用于产生注意力权重（选择操作），而值携带丰富的语义表示（值传递）。选择操作本质上是低维的，只需要O(log N)维度就能区分N个相关模式，因此对称设计是不必要的。

Method: 提出非对称注意力机制，降低查询和键的维度（d_select），保持值的高维度（d_model）。通过七个实验验证：1) 位置选择任务；2) 基于内容的检索；3-4) WikiText语言建模；5) GPT-2的SVD压缩；6) LLaMA模型验证；7) Mistral-7B的SVD压缩和QK微调。对现有模型采用SVD压缩后QK微调的方法。

Result: 实验表明：1) 位置选择任务仅需每头1维度；2) 内容检索需要~log₂N维度；3-4) WikiText语言建模中d_select = d_model/4仅增加4.3%困惑度，减少75% QK参数；5) GPT-2中键比查询更可压缩，轻量级QK微调几乎恢复所有质量损失；6) LLaMA模型验证了相同的退化比率；7) Mistral-7B实现75%键缓存节省，仅2.0%质量损失。对于7B参数模型服务128K上下文，非对称注意力每用户节省25GB KV缓存，使相同GPU上并发用户增加约60%。

Conclusion: 查询和键在注意力机制中本质上是低维的，非对称设计可以显著减少参数和KV缓存，同时保持模型质量。该方法为现有模型提供了实用的压缩方案，能显著提高推理效率和服务能力。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention uses identical dimensionality for queries, keys, and values ($d_q = d_k = d_v = \dmodel$). Our insight is that these components serve fundamentally different roles, and this symmetry is unnecessary. Queries and keys produce scalar attention weights (\emph{selection}), while values carry rich semantic representations (\emph{value transfer}). We argue that selection is an inherently lower-dimensional operation than value transfer, requiring only $\BigO(\log N)$ dimensions to distinguish among $N$ relevant patterns. We validate this hypothesis across seven experiments: (1)~positional selection tasks requiring just 1~dimension per head, (2)~content-based retrieval requiring $\sim\!\log_2 N$ dimensions, (3--4)~WikiText-2 and WikiText-103 language modeling where $\dselect = \dmodel/4$ incurs only 4.3\% perplexity increase while reducing QK parameters by 75\%, (5)~post-training SVD compression of GPT-2, revealing keys to be far more compressible than queries, with lightweight QK fine-tuning recovering nearly all quality loss, (6)~a 125M-parameter LLaMA model confirming identical degradation ratios across architectures, and (7)~Mistral-7B (7.2B parameters), where SVD compression followed by QK fine-tuning achieves 75\% key cache savings at just 2.0\% residual quality cost. For existing models, SVD compression followed by QK fine-tuning (3 epochs on a small fraction of pretraining data) achieves 75\% key cache savings at $<$2\% residual quality cost. For a 7B-parameter model serving 128K context, asymmetric attention saves 25\,GB of KV cache per user, enabling approximately 60\% more concurrent users on the same GPU.

</details>


### [222] [Agent Memory Below the Prompt: Persistent Q4 KV Cache for Multi-Agent LLM Inference on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04428)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于边缘设备多智能体LLM系统的KV缓存持久化方案，通过4位量化将缓存保存到磁盘并直接恢复，避免了重复的预填充计算，显著减少了首token生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备内存有限，无法同时容纳多个智能体的KV缓存，导致需要频繁驱逐和重新加载缓存，每次驱逐都需要完整的预填充计算（例如4K上下文需要15.7秒），严重影响多智能体系统的性能。

Method: 系统包含三个核心组件：1) 块池提供每个智能体隔离的4位量化KV缓存（safetensors格式）；2) BatchQuantizedKVCache支持多个智能体量化缓存的并发推理；3) 跨阶段上下文注入，无需重新计算即可在对话阶段间累积注意力状态。

Result: 在三种架构上评估：Gemma 3 12B、DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite 16B、Llama 3.1 8B。缓存恢复将首token生成时间减少了高达136倍（Gemma：4K-32K上下文下22-136倍；DeepSeek：11-76倍；Llama：24-111倍）。4位量化相比FP16在固定内存中可容纳4倍多的智能体上下文。困惑度变化：Gemma -0.7%，Llama +2.8%，DeepSeek +3.0%。

Conclusion: 通过将KV缓存持久化到磁盘并直接恢复，解决了边缘设备多智能体系统的内存瓶颈问题，显著减少了推理延迟，同时4位量化在可接受的精度损失下大幅提升了内存效率。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems on edge devices face a memory management problem: device RAM is too small to hold every agent's KV cache simultaneously. On Apple M4 Pro with 10.2 GB of cache budget, only 3 agents fit at 8K context in FP16. A 10-agent workflow must constantly evict and reload caches. Without persistence, every eviction forces a full re-prefill through the model -- 15.7 seconds per agent at 4K context. We address this by persisting each agent's KV cache to disk in 4-bit quantized format and reloading it directly into the attention layer, eliminating redundant O(n) prefill computation via direct cache restoration. The system comprises three components: a block pool providing per-agent isolated Q4 KV caches in safetensors format, a BatchQuantizedKVCache for concurrent inference over multiple agents' quantized caches, and cross-phase context injection that accumulates attention state across conversation phases without re-computation. Evaluated on three architectures (Gemma 3 12B, dense GQA, 48 layers; DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite 16B, MoE MLA, 27 layers; Llama 3.1 8B, dense GQA, 32 layers), cache restoration reduces time-to-first-token by up to 136x (Gemma: 22--136x at 4K--32K; DeepSeek: 11--76x at 4K--32K; Llama: 24--111x at 4K--16K; 3--10x at 1K). Q4 quantization fits 4x more agent contexts into fixed device memory than FP16. Perplexity measured with actual Q4 KV caches shows -0.7% for Gemma, +2.8% for Llama, and +3.0% for DeepSeek. Open-source at https://github.com/yshk-mxim/agent-memory

</details>


### [223] [Flowers: A Warp Drive for Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04430)
*Till Muser,Alexandra Spitzer,Matti Lassas,Maarten V. de Hoop,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flowers是一种完全由多头warp构建的PDE求解算子学习架构，不使用傅里叶乘子、点积注意力或卷积混合，通过预测位移场和warp混合输入特征实现自适应全局交互，计算成本线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE求解算子学习方法依赖傅里叶变换、注意力机制或卷积，计算成本高且可能不适合物理问题。Flowers旨在通过基于物理原理的warp机制实现高效、自适应的全局交互。

Method: Flowers完全基于多头warp：每个头预测位移场并warp混合输入特征；位移预测是点对点的，无需空间聚合；非局部性仅通过稀疏采样实现（每个头一个源坐标）；通过多尺度残差块堆叠warp形成完整架构。

Result: 在广泛的2D和3D时间相关PDE基准测试中表现优异，特别是在流体和波动问题上。紧凑的17M参数模型在相似规模下优于基于傅里叶、卷积和注意力的基线；150M参数变体优于参数更多、数据更多、训练计算更大的近期transformer基础模型。

Conclusion: Flowers通过基于warp的机制为PDE求解算子学习提供了高效、物理启发的替代方案，在保持线性计算成本的同时实现了自适应全局交互，在多个基准测试中超越了现有方法。

Abstract: We introduce Flowers, a neural architecture for learning PDE solution operators built entirely from multihead warps. Aside from pointwise channel mixing and a multiscale scaffold, Flowers use no Fourier multipliers, no dot-product attention, and no convolutional mixing. Each head predicts a displacement field and warps the mixed input features. Motivated by physics and computational efficiency, displacements are predicted pointwise, without any spatial aggregation, and nonlocality enters \emph{only} through sparse sampling at source coordinates, \emph{one} per head. Stacking warps in multiscale residual blocks yields Flowers, which implement adaptive, global interactions at linear cost. We theoretically motivate this design through three complementary lenses: flow maps for conservation laws, waves in inhomogeneous media, and a kinetic-theoretic continuum limit. Flowers achieve excellent performance on a broad suite of 2D and 3D time-dependent PDE benchmarks, particularly flows and waves. A compact 17M-parameter model consistently outperforms Fourier, convolution, and attention-based baselines of similar size, while a 150M-parameter variant improves over recent transformer-based foundation models with much more parameters, data, and training compute.

</details>


### [224] [ZorBA: Zeroth-order Federated Fine-tuning of LLMs with Heterogeneous Block Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04436)
*Chuiyang Meng,Ming Tang,Vincent W. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZorBA：基于零阶优化的联邦微调框架，通过异构块激活机制减少VRAM使用和通信开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调大语言模型面临两大挑战：1）本地更新需要大量VRAM存储梯度；2）频繁模型交换导致通信开销大。需要解决这些资源限制问题以实现高效的分布式协作微调。

Method: 提出ZorBA框架：1）采用零阶优化消除客户端梯度存储需求；2）异构块激活机制，服务器为不同客户端分配不同的Transformer块子集；3）使用共享随机种子和梯度有限差分减少通信开销；4）理论分析块激活决策对收敛速度和VRAM使用的影响；5）提出ε-约束字典序算法优化块激活决策。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）ZorBA在VRAM使用上比三个联邦微调基线方法最多减少62.41%；2）通信开销低；3）异构块激活机制有效加速收敛并减少资源消耗。

Conclusion: ZorBA通过零阶优化和异构块激活机制，有效解决了联邦微调大语言模型中的VRAM使用和通信开销问题，实现了资源高效的分布式模型微调。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) enables collaborative tuning across distributed clients. However, due to the large size of LLMs, local updates in federated learning (FL) may incur substantial video random-access memory (VRAM) usage. Moreover, frequent model exchange may lead to significant communication overhead. To tackle these challenges, in this paper we propose ZorBA, a zeroth-order optimization-based federated fine-tuning framework with heterogeneous block activation. ZorBA leverages zeroth-order optimization to eliminate the storage of gradients at the clients by forward passes. ZorBA includes a heterogeneous block activation mechanism in which the central server allocates different subsets of transformer blocks to clients in order to accelerate the convergence rate and reduce the VRAM usage. Furthermore, ZorBA utilizes shared random seeds and the finite differences of gradients in order to reduce the communication overhead. We conduct theoretical analysis to characterize the effect of block activation decisions on the convergence rate and VRAM usage. To jointly enhance the convergence rate and reduce the VRAM usage, we formulate an optimization problem to optimize the block activation decisions. We propose an $ε$-constraint lexicographic algorithm to solve this problem. Experimental results show that ZorBA outperforms three federated fine-tuning baselines in VRAM usage by up to 62.41% and incurs a low communication overhead.

</details>


### [225] [Learning Unified Distance Metric for Heterogeneous Attribute Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04458)
*Yiqun Zhang,Mingjie Zhao,Yizhou Chen,Yang Lu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HARR学习范式，通过将异构数值和分类属性投影到统一可学习的多空间中，实现混合数据的聚类分析，无需参数且收敛有保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实聚类任务中常包含数值和分类属性的混合数据。数值属性在欧氏空间有明确定义，而分类属性值代表不同概念且隐含在隐空间中。现有方法通常将异构属性编码为单一类型或定义统一度量，未能揭示它们的内在联系。

Method: 提出异构属性重构与表示学习范式，将异构属性转化为同质状态进行距离度量学习，并将学习与聚类集成以自动适应不同聚类任务。将每个属性的值投影到统一可学习的多空间中，更精细地表示和学习分类数据的距离度量。

Result: HARR方法无需参数、收敛有保证，能更有效地自适应不同聚类数k。大量实验证明其在准确性和效率方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过研究任意类型属性间的联系，提出的HARR学习范式能有效处理混合数据聚类问题，在保持高效的同时获得更好的聚类性能。

Abstract: Datasets composed of numerical and categorical attributes (also called mixed data hereinafter) are common in real clustering tasks. Differing from numerical attributes that indicate tendencies between two concepts (e.g., high and low temperature) with their values in well-defined Euclidean distance space, categorical attribute values are different concepts (e.g., different occupations) embedded in an implicit space. Simultaneously exploiting these two very different types of information is an unavoidable but challenging problem, and most advanced attempts either encode the heterogeneous numerical and categorical attributes into one type, or define a unified metric for them for mixed data clustering, leaving their inherent connection unrevealed. This paper, therefore, studies the connection among any-type of attributes and proposes a novel Heterogeneous Attribute Reconstruction and Representation (HARR) learning paradigm accordingly for cluster analysis. The paradigm transforms heterogeneous attributes into a homogeneous status for distance metric learning, and integrates the learning with clustering to automatically adapt the metric to different clustering tasks. Differing from most existing works that directly adopt defined distance metrics or learn attribute weights to search clusters in a subspace. We propose to project the values of each attribute into unified learnable multiple spaces to more finely represent and learn the distance metric for categorical data. HARR is parameter-free, convergence-guaranteed, and can more effectively self-adapt to different sought number of clusters $k$. Extensive experiments illustrate its superiority in terms of accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [226] [VSPrefill: Vertical-Slash Sparse Attention with Lightweight Indexing for Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04460)
*Chen Guanzhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VSPrefill：一种基于垂直-斜线结构模式的稀疏注意力机制，通过轻量级训练实现长上下文推理加速，在保持98.35%准确率的同时获得4.95倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力在预填充阶段的二次复杂度限制了大型语言模型的长上下文推理能力。现有稀疏注意力方法在上下文适应性、采样开销和微调成本之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出VSPrefill机制，使用垂直-斜线结构模式预测注意力分布。VSIndexer模块从增强RoPE的键值表示中预测垂直列和斜线对角线的重要性分数，构建线性复杂度的稀疏掩码而不修改主干参数。推理时采用自适应累积阈值策略分配每层稀疏预算，融合内核执行带实时索引合并的注意力。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct和LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上，使用LongBench和RULER基准测试，VSPrefill在128k上下文长度下保持98.35%的全注意力准确率，平均加速4.95倍。

Conclusion: VSPrefill在准确性和效率权衡中建立了新的帕累托前沿，为长上下文推理提供了有效的稀疏注意力解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of self-attention during the prefill phase impedes long-context inference in large language models. Existing sparse attention methods face a trade-off among context adaptivity, sampling overhead, and fine-tuning costs. We propose VSPrefill, a mechanism requiring lightweight training that uses the vertical-slash structural pattern in attention distributions. Our compact VSIndexer module predicts context-aware importance scores for vertical columns and slash diagonals from key-value representations augmented with RoPE. This approach constructs sparse masks with linear complexity without modifying the backbone parameters. During inference, an adaptive cumulative-threshold strategy allocates sparsity budgets per layer, while a fused kernel executes attention with on-the-fly index merging. Evaluated on Qwen3-4B-Instruct and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct across the LongBench and RULER benchmarks, VSPrefill preserves 98.35% of the full attention accuracy while delivering a 4.95x average speedup at a context length of 128k. These results establish a new Pareto frontier in the trade-off between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [227] [MAD-SmaAt-GNet: A Multimodal Advection-Guided Neural Network for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04461)
*Samuel van Wonderen,Siamak Mehrkanoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAD-SmaAt-GNet是一种用于降水临近预报的多模态物理引导深度学习模型，通过集成多天气变量编码器和基于物理的平流组件，相比基线SmaAt-UNet在4小时预报中将均方误差降低了8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值预报方法计算成本高且未能充分利用大量可用天气数据。深度学习模型在降水临近预报中显示出潜力，但现有CNN模型如SmaAt-UNet仍有改进空间，特别是在整合多模态天气数据和物理约束方面。

Method: 提出MAD-SmaAt-GNet模型，在SmaAt-UNet基础上进行两项扩展：(1) 增加额外编码器以学习多个天气变量（多模态输入）；(2) 集成基于物理的平流组件以确保预测的物理一致性。

Result: 每个扩展单独都能改善降雨预报，组合使用带来进一步增益。相比基线SmaAt-UNet，MAD-SmaAt-GNet在4步降水预报（最长4小时）中将均方误差降低了8.9%。多模态输入对短预报时效特别有益，而平流组件在短长期预报中都能提升性能。

Conclusion: MAD-SmaAt-GNet通过整合多模态天气数据和物理约束，显著提升了降水临近预报的准确性和物理一致性，证明了深度学习与物理知识结合的有效性。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting (short-term forecasting) is still often performed using numerical solvers for physical equations, which are computationally expensive and make limited use of the large volumes of available weather data. Deep learning models have shown strong potential for precipitation nowcasting, offering both accuracy and computational efficiency. Among these models, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are particularly effective for image-to-image prediction tasks. The SmaAt-UNet is a lightweight CNN based architecture that has demonstrated strong performance for precipitation nowcasting. This paper introduces the Multimodal Advection-Guided Small Attention GNet (MAD-SmaAt-GNet), which extends the core SmaAt-UNet by (i) incorporating an additional encoder to learn from multiple weather variables and (ii) integrating a physics-based advection component to ensure physically consistent predictions. We show that each extension individually improves rainfall forecasts and that their combination yields further gains. MAD-SmaAt-GNet reduces the mean squared error (MSE) by 8.9% compared with the baseline SmaAt-UNet for four-step precipitation forecasting up to four hours ahead. Additionally, experiments indicate that multimodal inputs are particularly beneficial for short lead times, while the advection-based component enhances performance across both short and long forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [228] [Oracle-efficient Hybrid Learning with Constrained Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04546)
*Princewill Okoroafor,Robert Kleinberg,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在混合在线学习问题中同时实现统计最优性和计算效率的新算法，通过约束对手从固定函数类中选择标签，利用ERM预言机高效运行并获得与Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合在线学习问题（特征从未知分布中i.i.d.抽取，标签由对手生成）处于统计学习和完全对抗在线学习之间。现有工作存在二分法：统计最优但计算不可行的算法，以及计算高效（给定ERM预言机）但统计次优的算法。本文旨在同时实现统计最优性和计算效率。

Method: 考虑结构化设置，约束对手从表达性强但固定的函数类R中选择标签。提出新的学习算法，在给定ERM预言机时高效运行，获得与从学习者假设类H和对手标签类R导出的类的Rademacher复杂度相关的遗憾界。技术工具包括：具有"截断熵正则化器"的新型Frank-Wolfe约简，以及"混合"鞅差序列和的新尾界。

Result: 主要结果是设计出在给定ERM预言机时高效运行的算法，其遗憾界与从H和R导出的类的Rademacher复杂度成比例。作为关键推论，为具有低维结构的高维动作集随机零和博弈提供了预言机高效的均衡计算算法。

Conclusion: 本文在混合学习设置中同时实现统计最优性和计算效率方面迈出了重要一步。通过约束对手的选择空间并开发新的技术工具，为结构化混合学习问题提供了有效的解决方案，并扩展了随机博弈均衡计算的应用。

Abstract: The Hybrid Online Learning Problem, where features are drawn i.i.d. from an unknown distribution but labels are generated adversarially, is a well-motivated setting positioned between statistical and fully-adversarial online learning. Prior work has presented a dichotomy: algorithms that are statistically-optimal, but computationally intractable (Wu et al., 2023), and algorithms that are computationally-efficient (given an ERM oracle), but statistically-suboptimal (Wu et al., 2024).
  This paper takes a significant step towards achieving statistical optimality and computational efficiency simultaneously in the Hybrid Learning setting. To do so, we consider a structured setting, where the Adversary is constrained to pick labels from an expressive, but fixed, class of functions $R$. Our main result is a new learning algorithm, which runs efficiently given an ERM oracle and obtains regret scaling with the Rademacher complexity of a class derived from the Learner's hypothesis class $H$ and the Adversary's label class $R$. As a key corollary, we give an oracle-efficient algorithm for computing equilibria in stochastic zero-sum games when action sets may be high-dimensional but the payoff function exhibits a type of low-dimensional structure. Technically, we develop a number of tools for the design and analysis of our learning algorithm, including a novel Frank-Wolfe reduction with "truncated entropy regularizer" and a new tail bound for sums of "hybrid" martingale difference sequences.

</details>


### [229] [Understanding the Dynamics of Demonstration Conflict in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04464)
*Difan Jiao,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在上下文学习中易受噪声演示影响，揭示了模型处理冲突证据的两阶段计算结构及关键注意力头的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习使大语言模型能够通过少量演示执行新任务，但演示本身可能包含噪声和冲突示例，使这种能力变得脆弱。为了理解模型如何处理此类冲突，研究模型在需要推断底层模式的任务中的表现。

Method: 使用线性探测和logit lens分析模型内部表示，识别处理冲突证据的两阶段计算结构。通过注意力头分析发现早期到中间层的"脆弱头"和晚层的"易受影响头"，并通过针对性消融验证发现。

Result: 模型在单个规则损坏的演示下性能显著下降。模型在中间层同时编码正确和错误规则，但在晚层才发展预测置信度。识别出的关键注意力头中，早期到中间层的"脆弱头"对损坏敏感，晚层的"易受影响头"显著减少对正确预测的支持。消融少量识别出的头可将性能提升超过10%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在上下文学习中处理冲突证据时表现出系统性的误导行为，揭示了模型内部的两阶段计算结构。通过识别和干预关键注意力头，可以显著提高模型对噪声演示的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In-context learning enables large language models to perform novel tasks through few-shot demonstrations. However, demonstrations per se can naturally contain noise and conflicting examples, making this capability vulnerable. To understand how models process such conflicts, we study demonstration-dependent tasks requiring models to infer underlying patterns, a process we characterize as rule inference. We find that models suffer substantial performance degradation from a single demonstration with corrupted rule. This systematic misleading behavior motivates our investigation of how models process conflicting evidence internally.
